# Visual Studio Code Release Archive

A verified index of historical stable Visual Studio Code releases and first-party download links.

Generated/verified: 2026-10-27

Every listed download was checked with an HTTP GET (a byte range where supported), followed through redirects, checked for an artifact response and package signature, and associated with the requested version. Failed or unavailable downloads are omitted. Source links are evidence, not download links.

## Sources and verification method

The candidate set was merged from Microsoft?s stable-release and commit APIs, the Microsoft release-notes archive, and the official microsoft/vscode GitHub releases and tags. Version-specific download endpoints were requested; a failed package endpoint is not treated as proof that a release did not exist. See the accompanying JSON audit for probes, redirect chains, artifact metadata, and the independent final-document link audit.

- [Microsoft stable-release API](https://update.code.visualstudio.com/api/releases/stable)
- [Microsoft stable commit API](https://update.code.visualstudio.com/api/commits/stable)
- [Microsoft release-notes archive](https://code.visualstudio.com/updates/archive)
- [microsoft/vscode releases](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/releases)
- [microsoft/vscode tags](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/tags)
